# RAW-REM-01 Source Uncertainty Triage

## 1. Current Truth Statement

- Raw V1 source logic coverage currently FAILS.
- This package addresses only the 234 `V1LOGIC_SOURCE_UNCERTAIN_FAIL` items.
- It does not address the 6,457 `V1LOGIC_UNCOVERED_FAIL` items.
- It does not fix full raw logic coverage.
- It does not authorize implementation.
- 02M remains frozen.

## 2. RAW-REM-01 Scope

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Baseline commit | `541b90a` |
| Failure commit | `ad5909a` |
| Source uncertainty baseline | 234 |
| Target package family | `RAW-REM-01` |
| Input source | `V1_SOURCE_RAW_LOGIC_COVERAGE_AUDIT.md` |
| Output decision file | `RAW_REM_01_SOURCE_UNCERTAINTY_DECISIONS.md` |

## 3. Source Uncertainty Triage Method

| Allowed outcome | Definition |
|---|---|
| SOURCE_CONFIRMED_FOR_FUTURE_MAPPING | The V1 source reference is sufficiently grounded for mapping in a later RAW-REM package. |
| SOURCE_FALSE_POSITIVE_WITH_REASON | Over-broad extraction created an item that is not an independent logic unit; an explicit reason is mandatory. |
| SOURCE_STILL_UNCERTAIN_BLOCKED | Source uncertainty remains and blocks full planning completeness. |
| SOURCE_REQUIRES_MANUAL_ARCHIVE_REVIEW | Available automated evidence cannot resolve source-unit semantics; named manual archive/source review is required. |
| SOURCE_NOT_APPLICABLE_WITH_REASON | Source-grounded evidence establishes that the item is outside V2 planning scope. |

Method: start from the 234 original audit rows, locate each exact archive entry, read it fully, record SHA256 and format-specific structural evidence, and preserve blocking status whenever independent logic-unit semantics cannot be proven. Readability is not treated as semantic certainty.
